never embeds a course's identity. Two\\neligibility shapes are supported, deliberately not merged into one\\n\\\"guess it\\\" heuristic:\\n\\n- ``explicit_assignment_ids``: an exact allowlist. Use this once a course's\\n  grading policy for an assignment is battery-evidenced enough to trust for\\n  live writeback -- CS1 assignment 151 (prompt 103's proven canary) is the\\n  only entry that starts this way.\\n- ``discover_rubric_backed=True``: eligibility is computed at poll time from\\n  Canvas's own assignment list (an assignment counts if Harbor's\\n  ``list_assignments`` shows it has a rubric attached). This is the safe\\n  default for every course that hasn't been explicitly reviewed yet -- it\\n  finds new pod content automatically without the listener ever grading an\\n  assignment nobody attached a rubric to, and it can only run in\\n  ``dispatch_mode=\\\"dry_run\\\"`` (enforced in ``__post_init__``) so a rubric\\n  landing in a course never silently flips on live writeback.\\n\\n``dispatch_mode=\\\"dry_run\\\"`` means discovery + routing decisions happen and\\nare logged, but ``run_grading_pipeline`` is always called with ``live=False``\\n-- Canvas never receives a comment.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nfrom collections.abc import Callable\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\\n\\nfrom course_foundry.cs1_ladder_default_scope import cs1_ladder_scope_reason\\n\\nDispatchMode = str  # unreachable -- run_forever loops until killed\\n\\n\\ndef cmd_status(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:\\n    state = ListenerState(args.state_db)\\n    rows = state.all_rows(status=args.status)\\n    for row in rows:\\n        print(f\\\"{row.item_id}\\\\tstatus={row.status}\\\\tlane={row.compute_lane}\\\\tretries={row.retry_count}\\\\terror={row.last_error}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n{len(rows)} row(s)\\\" + (f\\\" with status={args.status}\\\" if args.status else \\\"\\\"))\\n    state.close()\\n    return 0\\n\\n\\ndef cmd_health(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:\\n    from course_foundry.submission_listener.gpu import check_t4_available\\n    evidence = check_t4_available()\\n    print(json.dumps(evidence.__dict__, indent=2))\\n    try:\\n        client = _build_client()\\n        client.get(\\\"/api/v1/accounts/1/courses?per_page=1\\\")\\n        print(\\\"canvas: reachable\\\")\\n    except Exception as exc:\\n        print(f\\\"canvas: UNREACHABLE ({exc})\\\")\\n        return 1\\n    return 0\\n\\n\\ndef build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\\\"submission-listener\\\")\\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\\\"command\\\", required=True)\\n\\n    p_once = sub.add_parser(\\\"once\\\", help=\\\"Single discover+dispatch pass, then exit. Polls\\nevery registered Savnac course (`course_foundry/course_foundry/submission_listener/config.py`),\\nclaims new eligible submission attempts durably (SQLite), routes each to\\nlocal T4 or NRP, and calls the existing `run_grading_pipeline`. Course 1\\n(CS1, assignment 151 — the prompt 103-proven canary) is the only course\\nconfigured `dispatch_mode=\\\"live\\\"` today; the other six Savnac courses are\\nregistered for `dry_run` discovery only until a reviewed rubric/policy\\nexists for them. Flipping a course to live is a one-line edit in\\n`config.py`, not new code.\\n\\n## Install / verify (preferred)\\n\\n```bash\\nsudo bash deploy/submission_listener/install_or_verify.sh\\n```\\n\\nIdempotent and recon-first: safe to re-run any time. It checks what's\\nactually running before touching anything, refuses to create a second\\nlistener process, tries a no-sudo-required `systemd --user` install first,\\nfalls back to the system-level unit below only if that doesn't come up\\nhealthy, and writes a timestamped report under `reports/` plus one line to\\n`deploy/submission_listener/INSTALL_LOG.md` every run. See that log's header\\nfor why this script exists — a hand-pasted `systemd --user` install on\\n2026-08-15 got corrupted mid-paste and briefly crash-looped a second\\nlistener process alongside the already-running system-level one.\\n\\n### Manual install (system-level, what the script falls back to)\\n\\n```bash\\nmkdir -p /mnt/brandy_nvme/jevert/git/course_foundry/runs/submission_listener/receipts\\nsudo cp deploy/submission_listener/submission-listener.service /etc/systemd/system/\\nsudo systemctl daemon-reload\\nsudo systemctl enable submission-listener\\nsudo systemctl start submission-listener\\n```\\n\\nPrefer the script above over doing this by hand.\\n\\n`EnvironmentFile` points at `~/.config/canvas/savnac.env` explicitly (never\\nthe default `canvas.env`, which points at real production\\n`swosu.instructure.com` — see the repo-wide convention this follows).\\n\\nOn Brandy, the git volume is mounted `noexec`, so the unit deliberately uses\\nthe host Python 3.11 interpreter and an explicit `PYTHONPATH` covering the\\nchecked-out editable dependencies and the Course Foundry virtualenv's\\nsite-packages. Do not replace this with the virtualenv's `bin/python`.\\nThe unit also sets an explicit `PATH` including `~/.local/bin`, where Brandy's\\n`kubectl` lives; systemd does not inherit the interactive shell PATH.\\n\\n## Operate\\n\\n```bash\\n# status / logs\\nsudo systemctl status submission-listener\\njournalctl -u submission-listener -f\\n\\n# stop / restart\\nsudo systemctl stop submission-listener\\nsudo systemctl restart submission-listener\\n\\n# claim-state table (all rows, or filter by status)\\ncd /mnt/brandy_nvme/jevert/git/course_foundry && source .venv/bin/activate\\npython -m course_foundry.submission_listener.cli status --state-db runs/submission_listener/state.sqlite3\\npython -m course_foundry.submission_listener.cli status --state-db runs/submission_listener/state.sqlite3 --status human_review\\n\\n# GPU + Canvas reachability check\\npython -m course_foundry.submission_listener.cli health\\n\\n# single debugging pass (discover + dispatch once, print summary, exit) --\\n# safe: omit --live for a dry run that discovers but never writes to Canvas\\npython -m course_foundry.submission_listener.cli once --state-db runs/submission_listener/state.sqlite3 \\\\\\n    --receipts-dir runs/submission_listener/receipts \\\\\\n    --alias-table-path runs/submission_listener/receipts/pii_alias_table.json\\n```\\n\\nConfig path: `course_foundry/course_foundry/submission_listener/config.py`\\n(`SAVNAC_COURSES`). production course and build its local plan.\"\"\"\n+    try:\n+        spec = PRODUCTION_COURSE_REGISTRY[course]\n+    except KeyError as exc:\n+        raise DeploymentError(f\"unknown production course {course!r}\") from exc\n+    if course_id is not None and course_id != spec.course_id:\n+        raise DeploymentError(\n+            f\"course {course!r} is restricted to course id require_host_marker(config.api_base_url, PRODUCTION_HOST_MARKER, tool_name=\"production deployer\")\n+    course_only = CanvasConfig(config.api_base_url, config.api_token, frozenset({course_id}))\n+
